Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electricity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 852. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 698 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_852(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 852. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^698(S, F) is trivial. QED.
